  /* Dead Client: Rise-style layout. Builds the left sidebar from the category

  function deadRiseLayout() {
    try {
      const root = document.getElementById("custom-gui-container");
      if (!root) return;
      const panels = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll(":scope > .gui-panel"));
      if (!panels.length) return;
      const old = root.querySelector(".dead-sidebar");
      if (old) old.remove();
      const icons = {
        Combat: "\u2694",
        Movement: "\u27A4",
        World: "\u25C9",
        Visual: "\u25C8",
      };
      const names = panels.map(
        (p) => (p.querySelector(".gui-header span") || {}).textContent || "",
      );
      let cur = window.__deadActiveCat;
      if (!names.includes(cur)) cur = names[0];
      window.__deadActiveCat = cur;

      const side = document.createElement("div");
      side.className = "dead-sidebar";
      const logo = document.createElement("div");
      logo.className = "dead-logo";
      logo.innerHTML = "Dead";
      side.appendChild(logo);
      const nav = document.createElement("div");
      nav.className = "dead-nav";
      side.appendChild(nav);

      const select = (name) => {
        window.__deadActiveCat = name;
        panels.forEach((p, i) => p.classList.toggle("dead-active", names[i] === name));
        nav.querySelectorAll(".dead-nav-item").forEach((n) =>
          n.classList.toggle("active", n.dataset.cat === name),
        );
      };
      names.forEach((name) => {
        const it = document.createElement("div");
        it.className = "dead-nav-item";
        it.dataset.cat = name;
        const ic = document.createElement("span");
        ic.className = "dead-nav-ico";
        ic.textContent = icons[name] || "\u25CF";
        const tx = document.createElement("span");
        tx.textContent = name;
        it.appendChild(ic);
        it.appendChild(tx);
        it.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          select(name);
        });
        nav.appendChild(it);
      });
      const user = document.createElement("div");
      user.className = "dead-user";
      user.innerHTML =
        '<div class="dead-avatar">D</div><div><b>Dead</b><small>Dead Client</small></div>';
      side.appendChild(user);
      root.insertBefore(side, root.firstChild);
      select(cur);
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  function ensureDeadClientCloseButton() {
    if (!Nxmmb || Nxmmb.querySelector(".dead-client-close")) return;
    const close = document.createElement("button");
    close.type = "button";

export default {

  async fetch(request) {

    const url = new URL(request.url);

    const targetUrl = TARGET + url.pathname + url.search;

 

    // İsteği bloxd.io'ya yönlendir

    const headers = new Headers(request.headers);

    headers.set("Host", new URL(TARGET).host);

    headers.set("Origin", TARGET);

    headers.set("Referer", TARGET + "/");

 

    // WebSocket isteklerini olduğu gibi geçir

    if (request.headers.get("Upgrade") === "websocket") {

      return fetch(targetUrl, new Request(request, { headers }));

    }

 

    const response = await fetch(targetUrl, {

      method: request.method,

      headers,

      body: ["GET", "HEAD"].includes(request.method) ? null : request.body,

      redirect: "manual",

    });

 

    // Güvenlik başlıklarını kaldır (enjekte kodun engellenmemesi için)

    const newHeaders = new Headers(response.headers);

    newHeaders.delete("content-security-policy");

    newHeaders.delete("content-security-policy-report-only");

    newHeaders.delete("x-frame-options");

 

    const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";

 

    // Sadece HTML sayfasına kod enjekte et

    if (contentType.includes("text/html")) {

      return new HTMLRewriter()

        .on("head", {

          element(el) {

            el.prepend(`<script>${CUSTOM_JS}</script>`, { html: true });

          },

        })

        .transform(new Response(response.body, {

          status: response.status,

          headers: newHeaders,

        }));

    }

 

    return new Response(response.body, {

      status: response.status,

      headers: newHeaders,

    });

  },

};
